- `matact-triple-left-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k)) = entry(matact(md, matmul(scal(md), p, q), u), row, col)))
- `matact-triple-right` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matact(md, p, matact(md, q, u)), row, col) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
- `matact-triple-right-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n)) = entry(matact(md, p, matact(md, q, u)), row, col)))
- `matact-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, q, p, u], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, q, vec(md)) implies (n = 0 implies m = 0 or q = 0) implies matact(md, p, u) in mat(m, q, vec(md))))
- `module-act-distrib-scalar` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-distrib-scalar-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_)))
- `module-act-distrib-vec` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `module-act-distrib-vec-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_)) = (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_))))
- `module-act-mul-compat` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-mul-compat-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_)))
- `module-act-neg-one` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md)], (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(one(scal(md))), x) = (vneg(md))(x)))
- `module-act-neg-one-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md)], (vneg(md))(x) = (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(one(scal(md))), x)))
- `module-act-type` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `module-act-unital` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(one(scal(m)), x_) = x_))
- `module-act-unital-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], x_ = (act(m))(one(scal(m)), x_)))
- `module-act-zero-vec` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md))], (act(md))(r, vzero(md)) = vzero(md)))
- `module-act-zero-vec-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md))], vzero(md) = (act(md))(r, vzero(md))))
- `module-scalar-ring` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies is-ring(scal(m)))
- `module-scalar-zero-in` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies zero(scal(m)) in carr(scal(m)))
- `module-vadd-type` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in vec(m)))
- `module-vector-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies is-abelian-group(module-vector-ag(r)))
- `module-vneg-type` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))(x_) in vec(m)))
- `module-vzero-in` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies vzero(m) in vec(m))
- `module-zero-act` — forall([s], is-module(s) implies forall([x in vec(s)], (act(s))(zero(scal(s)), x) = vzero(s)))
- `module-zero-act-rev` — forall([s], is-module(s) implies forall([x in vec(s)], vzero(s) = (act(s))(zero(scal(s)), x)))
- `mra-combined-summand-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, c1, c2, u], c1 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies c2 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (opr(module-vector-ag(md)))((vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c1, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z), (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c2, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mrs-scaled-summand-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, r, c, u], r in carr(scal(md)) implies c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (act(md))(r, (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mvag-carr` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies carr(module-vector-ag(md)) = vec(md))
- `mvag-carr-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies vec(md) = carr(module-vector-ag(md)))
- `mvag-id` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies iden(module-vector-ag(md)) = vzero(md))
- `mvag-id-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies vzero(md) = iden(module-vector-ag(md)))
- `mvag-op` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies opr(module-vector-ag(md)) = vadd(md))
- `mvag-op-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies vadd(md) = opr(module-vector-ag(md)))

- `spans-span` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, n, u, span(md, n, u))))
- `spans-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u, pm, sm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), n, pm) implies is-submodule(md, sm) implies spans(md, n, u, sm) implies spans(md, n, matact(md, pm, u), sm)))
- `submodule-finsum-closed` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(md)))], forall([z in s], f(z) in sm) implies finsum(module-vector-ag(md), f, s) in sm))))
- `submodule-intersection` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([s1], is-submodule(md, s1) implies forall([s2], is-submodule(md, s2) implies is-submodule(md, intersection(s1, s2)))))
- `whole-module-is-submodule` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-submodule(md, vec(md)))

- `normed-vector-space-as-module` — from `normed-vector-space`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)

### monoid
<a id="monoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/monoid.scm`](../structure-library/monoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): carriers (carr), ops/constants (opr iden)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure monoid
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-monoid` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-monoid`.*

- `hom-monoid-id` — forall([a], is-monoid(a) implies is-hom-monoid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `monoid-assoc` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `monoid-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))
- `monoid-carrier-is-set` — forall([m_], is-monoid(m_) implies carr(m_) in set)
- `monoid-identity-in` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies iden(m) in carr(m))
- `monoid-left-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `monoid-left-id-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(iden(s), a)))
- `monoid-right-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))
- `monoid-right-id-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(a, iden(s))))
- `mpow-add` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(m, x, j + k) = (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k))))
- `mpow-add-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), j in nn, k in nn], (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k)) = mpow(m, x, j + k)))
- `mpow-one` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], mpow(m, x, 1) = x))
- `mpow-one-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], x = mpow(m, x, 1)))
- `mpow-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), n in nn], mpow(m, x, n) in carr(m)))
- `prod-ord-singleton` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m))], prod-ord(m, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `prod-ord-singleton-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m))], f(0) = prod-ord(m, f, 1)))
- `prod-ord-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m)), n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, n) in carr(m)))

*Views into `monoid`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid` — from `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — from `ring`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)

### nn-add-monoid
<a id="nn-add-monoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/numeric-instances.scm`](../structure-library/numeric-instances.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`comm-monoid`](#comm-monoid).

*Member of:*

- `is-comm-monoid` (via `nn-add-monoid-is-comm-monoid`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `comm-monoid` slots):

- `carr` = `nn`
- `opr` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_)`
- `iden` = `0`

### normed-ag
<a id="normed-ag"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-ag.scm`](../structure-library/normed-ag.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (5): carriers (carr), ops/constants (opr iden inv nrm)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-ag
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (op inv carr carr)
  (op nrm carr rr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr)
  (property has-inverses opr iden inv carr)
  (property is-commutative opr carr)
  (property is-group-norm nrm opr inv iden carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-normed-ag` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) iff length(s) = 5 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-group-norm(nrm(s), opr(s), inv(s), iden(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-normed-ag`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-normed-ag(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(r))))
- `hom-normed-ag-id` — forall([a], is-normed-ag(a) implies is-hom-normed-ag(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `nag-metric-distance` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies forall([u in carr(nag), v in carr(nag)], (dist(nag-metric-space(nag)))(u, v) = (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v)))))
- `nag-metric-distance-rev` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies forall([u in carr(nag), v in carr(nag)], (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v))) = (dist(nag-metric-space(nag)))(u, v)))
- `nag-metric-space-is-metric-space` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies is-metric-space(nag-metric-space(nag)))
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-normed-ag(r) implies is-abelian-group(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(r)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (nrm(s))(a) = 0 iff a = iden(s)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite-rev` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = iden(s) iff (nrm(s))(a) = 0))
- `normed-ag-nrm-nonneg` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], 0 <= (nrm(s))(a)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-subadditive` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (nrm(s))((opr(s))(a, b)) <= (nrm(s))(a) + (nrm(s))(b)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-type` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr))
- `sums-to-unique` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)), r1 in carr(grp), r2 in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r1) and sums-to(grp, f, r2) implies r1 = r2))
- `sums-to-unique-rev` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)), r1 in carr(grp), r2 in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r1) and sums-to(grp, f, r2) implies r2 = r1))

*Views into `normed-ag`.*

- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — from `normed-vector-space`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)

*Views from `normed-ag`.*

- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — into `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

### normed-field
<a id="normed-field"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-field.scm`](../structure-library/normed-field.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): carriers (carr), ops/constants (add mul neg zero one fnrm)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-field
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (op fnrm carr rr)
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-commutative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr)
  (property is-norm fnrm add mul zero carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-normed-field` — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and fnrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-norm(fnrm(s), add(s), mul(s), zero(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-normed-field`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-commutative-ring-additive-ag-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(commutative-ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-field-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-field-additive-ag(r))))

### normed-vector-space
<a id="normed-vector-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-vector-space.scm`](../structure-library/normed-vector-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): carriers (vec), ops/constants (vadd vzero vneg act vnrm)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-vector-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (op vnrm vec rr)
  (property is-associative vadd vec)
  (property is-commutative vadd vec)
  (property is-identity vadd vzero vec)
  (property has-inverses vadd vzero vneg vec)
  (law "scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(r_, vadd(s)(x_, y_)) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(r_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(add(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(mul(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = act(s)(r_, act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], act(s)(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_)")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= vnrm(s)(x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s)(x_) = 0) iff (x_ = vzero(s)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          vnrm(s)(act(s)(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * vnrm(s)(x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          vnrm(s)(vadd(s)(x_, y_)) <= vnrm(s)(x_) + vnrm(s)(y_))"))
```

- `nvs-act-collect` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, x_)), (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_ + s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-collect-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_ + s_, x_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, x_)), (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-scalar` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-scalar-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_)))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_)) = (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec-rr` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec-rr-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_)) = (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-act-mul-compat` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-mul-compat-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_)))
- `nvs-act-one` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(1, x_)) = (vadd(m))(y_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-one-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, x_) = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(1, x_))))
- `nvs-act-scale-assoc` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))(r_ * s_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-scale-assoc-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_ * s_, x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-type` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-act-unital` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(one(scal(m)), x_) = x_))
- `nvs-act-unital-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], x_ = (act(m))(one(scal(m)), x_)))
- `nvs-act-vneg` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = (act(m))(-1 * r_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-vneg-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(-1 * r_, x_) = (vneg(m))((act(m))(r_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(0, x_)) = y_))
- `nvs-act-zero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], y_ = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(0, x_))))
- `nvs-dist-shift` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, (vadd(m))(u_, w_)) = (vnrm(m))(w_)))
- `nvs-dist-shift-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))(w_) = (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, (vadd(m))(u_, w_))))
- `nvs-metric-distance` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_) = (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_)))))
- `nvs-metric-distance-q` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_) == (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_)))))
- `nvs-metric-distance-q-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_))) == (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_)))
- `nvs-metric-distance-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_))) = (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_)))
- `nvs-metric-is-ms` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-metric-space(nvs-metric-space(m)))
- `nvs-neg-one-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(-1, x_) = (vneg(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-neg-one-act-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))(x_) = (act(m))(-1, x_)))
- `nvs-neg-one-act-sub` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(0 - 1, u_) = (vneg(m))(u_)))
- `nvs-neg-one-act-sub-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))(u_) = (act(m))(0 - 1, u_)))
- `nvs-norm-one-term-bound` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([v1 in vec(m), v2 in vec(m), c1 in rr, bd in rr], 0 <= bd implies abs(c1) <= bd implies (vnrm(m))((act(m))(c1, v1)) <= bd * (1 + ((vnrm(m))(v2) + (vnrm(m))(v1)))))
- `nvs-norm-two-term-bound` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([v1 in vec(m), v2 in vec(m), c1 in rr, c2 in rr, bd in rr], 0 <= bd implies abs(c1) <= bd implies abs(c2) <= bd implies (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))((act(m))(c1, v1), (act(m))(c2, v2))) <= bd * (1 + ((vnrm(m))(v2) + (vnrm(m))(v1)))))
- `nvs-one-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(1, x_) = x_))
- `nvs-one-act-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], x_ = (act(m))(1, x_)))
- `nvs-scal-add` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies add(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_))
- `nvs-scal-add-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_) == add(scal(m)))
- `nvs-scal-carr` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies carr(scal(m)) == rr)
- `nvs-scal-carr-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies rr == carr(scal(m)))
- `nvs-scal-mul` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies mul(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_))
- `nvs-scal-mul-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_) == mul(scal(m)))
- `nvs-scal-neg` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies neg(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_))
- `nvs-scal-neg-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_) == neg(scal(m)))
- `nvs-scal-one` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies one(scal(m)) == 1)
- `nvs-scal-one-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies 1 == one(scal(m)))
- `nvs-scal-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies zero(scal(m)) == 0)
- `nvs-scal-zero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies 0 == zero(scal(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-assoc` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(u_, v_), w_) = (vadd(m))(u_, (vadd(m))(v_, w_))))
- `nvs-vadd-assoc-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, (vadd(m))(v_, w_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(u_, v_), w_)))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = (vadd(m))(x_, w_) implies z_ = w_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = (vadd(m))(x_, w_) implies w_ = z_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(z_, x_) = (vadd(m))(w_, x_) implies z_ = w_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(z_, x_) = (vadd(m))(w_, x_) implies w_ = z_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = x_ implies z_ = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-zero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = x_ implies vzero(m) = z_))
- `nvs-vadd-comm` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, w_) = (vadd(m))(w_, u_)))
- `nvs-vadd-comm-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(w_, u_) = (vadd(m))(u_, w_)))
- `nvs-vadd-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-shuffle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([a_ in vec(m), b_ in vec(m), c_ in vec(m), d_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, c_), (vadd(m))(b_, d_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, b_), (vadd(m))(c_, d_))))
- `nvs-vadd-shuffle-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([a_ in vec(m), b_ in vec(m), c_ in vec(m), d_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, b_), (vadd(m))(c_, d_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, c_), (vadd(m))(b_, d_))))
- `nvs-vneg-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))(x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(u_), u_) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-left-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(u_), u_)))
- `nvs-vneg-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(u_)) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(u_))))
- `nvs-vneg-vadd` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(x_), (vneg(m))(y_))))
- `nvs-vneg-vadd-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(x_), (vneg(m))(y_)) = (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-c` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-c-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_) = (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_) = (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-triangle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(m))(x_) + (vnrm(m))(y_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-vzero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies (vnrm(m))(vzero(m)) = 0)
- `nvs-vnrm-vzero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies 0 = (vnrm(m))(vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vzero-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vzero(m) in vec(m))
- `nvs-vzero-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(vzero(m), u_) = u_))
- `nvs-vzero-left-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], u_ = (vadd(m))(vzero(m), u_)))
- `nvs-vzero-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, vzero(m)) = u_))
- `nvs-vzero-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], u_ = (vadd(m))(u_, vzero(m))))
- `nvs-zero-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(0, x_) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-zero-act-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (act(m))(0, x_)))
- `seg-curve-diff-at` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([f in fun(vec(m), rr), b_ in vec(m), eta in vec(m), s_ in rr, k_], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, (vadd(m))(b_, (act(m))(s_, eta)), eta, k_) implies is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, b_, eta), s_, k_)))
- `vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)

*Views from `normed-vector-space`.*

- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — into `normed-ag`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)
- `normed-vector-space-as-module` — into `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)

### pid
<a id="pid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ideal.scm`](../structure-library/ideal.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as integral-domain)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`integral-domain`](#integral-domain).  Shape inherited from `integral-domain` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pid
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as integral-domain)
  (law "forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies
          forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pid-def` — forall([s], is-pid(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pid`.*

- `hom-pid-id` — forall([a], is-pid(a) implies is-hom-pid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))

### pseudometric-space
<a id="pseudometric-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/pseudometric.scm`](../structure-library/pseudometric.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): carriers (pts), ops/constants (dist)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pseudometric-space
  (carriers pts)
  (op dist (cartesian pts pts) rr)
  (property is-pseudometric dist pts))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pseudometric-space` — forall([s], is-pseudometric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-pseudometric(dist(s), pts(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pseudometric-space`.*

- `hom-pseudometric-space-id` — forall([a], is-pseudometric-space(a) implies is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))

### qq-field
<a id="qq-field"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/numeric-instances.scm`](../structure-library/numeric-instances.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`field`](#field).

*Member of:*

- `is-field` (via `qq-field-is-field`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `field` slots):

- `carr` = `qq`
- `add` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)`
- `mul` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)`
- `neg` = `vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)`
- `zero` = `0`
- `one` = `1`
- `non-zero` = `difference(qq, singleton(0))`
- `mul-inv` = `vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))`

### qq-line
<a id="qq-line"></a>

*Declared in* [`theorem-library/qq-vs-exemplification.scm`](../theorem-library/qq-vs-exemplification.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`vector-space`](#vector-space).

*Member of:*

- `is-module` (via `qq-line-is-module`)
- `is-vector-space` (via `qq-line-is-vector-space`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `vector-space` slots):

- `scal` = `field-as-integral-domain(qq-field)`
- `vec` = `qq`
- `vadd` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)`
- `vzero` = `0`
- `vneg` = `vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)`
- `act` = `vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)`

### qq-ring
<a id="qq-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/numeric-instances.scm`](../structure-library/numeric-instances.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`integral-domain`](#integral-domain).

*Member of:*

- `is-integral-domain` (via `qq-is-integral-domain`)
- `is-commutative-ring` (via `qq-is-commutative-ring`)
- `is-ring` (via `qq-is-ring`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `integral-domain` slots):

- `carr` = `qq`
- `add` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)`
- `mul` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)`
- `neg` = `vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)`
- `zero` = `0`
- `one` = `1`

### ring
<a id="ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ring.scm`](../structure-library/ring.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (6): carriers (carr), ops/constants (add mul neg zero one)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure ring
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-ring` — forall([s], is-ring(s) iff length(s) = 6 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-ring`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(ring-additive-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))
- `abelian-group-is-group-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-group(ring-additive-ag(r)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(b, a) = (add(r))(a, b)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(b, a) = (add(r))(a, b)))
- `ag-cancel-right-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies b = a))
- `ag-cancel-right-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies b = a))
- `border-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p) = identmat(a, succ(p))))
- `border-identity-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], identmat(a, succ(p)) = border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p)))

- `border-is-diagonal` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, e, p, q], p in nn implies q in nn implies is-diagonal(a, p, q, e) implies b in carr(a) implies e in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies is-diagonal(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `border-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, r, x, y, b, d], p in nn implies q in nn implies r in nn implies x in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies y in mat(q, r, carr(a)) implies b in carr(a) implies d in carr(a) implies (q = 0 implies p = 0 or r = 0) implies matmul(a, border(a, b, x, p, q), border(a, d, y, q, r)) = border(a, (mul(a))(b, d), matmul(a, x, y), p, r)))

- `identmat-left-identity-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], p = matmul(a, identmat(a, m), p)))

- `mat-ring-mul-fun` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matmul(a, p, q)) in fun(cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), mat(n, n, carr(a)))))

- `matmul-assoc-summand-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))) in fun(cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `matmul-entry` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, p, q], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, k)], entry(matmul(a, p, q), i, c) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, j, c))), interval(1, n)))))

- `matscale-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, r, p], r in carr(a) implies p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies matscale(a, r, p) in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `matunit-col-shift` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, matunit(a, n, k, l)), i, c) = if(c = l, entry(p, i, k), zero(a)))))
- `matunit-col-shift-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], if(c = l, entry(p, i, k), zero(a)) = entry(matmul(a, p, matunit(a, n, k, l)), i, c))))

*Views into `ring`.*

- `ringoid-as-ring` — from `ringoid`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)

*Views from `ring`.*

- `ring-additive-ag` — into `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — into `monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)

### ringoid
<a id="ringoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ringoid.scm`](../structure-library/ringoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): carriers (carr), ops/constants (add mul neg zero one idl)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure ringoid
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (constant idl (power carr))
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr)
  (property is-ideal-in idl carr add mul neg zero))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-ringoid` — forall([s], is-ringoid(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and idl(s) in power(carr(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-ideal-in(idl(s), carr(s), add(s), mul(s), neg(s), zero(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-ringoid`.*

- `hom-ringoid-id` — forall([a], is-ringoid(a) implies is-hom-ringoid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `r6b-add-respects2` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))))
- `r6b-radd-interchange` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, c, d], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies d in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (add(r))(c, d)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, c), (add(r))(b, d))))
- `r6b-radd-interchange-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, c, d], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies d in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, c), (add(r))(b, d)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (add(r))(c, d))))
- `r6b-radd-right-id` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, zero(r)) = a))
- `r6b-radd-right-id-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], a = (add(r))(a, zero(r))))
- `r6b-radd-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `r6b-rdiff-add` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((add(r))(u, v))) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v)))))
- `r6b-rdiff-add-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v))) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((add(r))(u, v)))))
- `r6b-related-in` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b)))
- `r6b-related-out` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r)))

### semigroup
<a id="semigroup"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/semigroup.scm`](../structure-library/semigroup.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): carriers (carr), ops/constants (opr)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure semigroup
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-semigroup` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) iff length(s) = 2 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-semigroup`.*

- `hom-semigroup-id` — forall([a], is-semigroup(a) implies is-hom-semigroup(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `semigroup-assoc` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `semigroup-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))

### setoid
<a id="setoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/setoid.scm`](../structure-library/setoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): carriers (pts rel), ops/constants ()

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure setoid
  (carriers pts rel)
  (property is-equivalence rel pts))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-setoid` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and rel(s) in set and is-equivalence(rel(s), pts(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-setoid`.*

- `class-disjoint` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) or intersection(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = empty-set))
- `class-eq-iff` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) iff related(s, a, b)))
- `class-eq-iff-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], related(s, a, b) iff class(s, a) = class(s, b)))
- `class-in-quotient` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in quotient(s)))
- `class-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in set))
- `class-self` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], a in class(s, a)))
- `class-subset-carrier` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) subset pts(s)))
- `descend-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], (descend(s, f))(class(s, a)) = f(a))))
- `descend-computes-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], f(a) = (descend(s, f))(class(s, a)))))
- `descend-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies descend(s, f) in fun(quotient(s), z)))
- `descend2-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies (descend2(s, f))(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = f(a, b))))
- `descend2-computes-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies f(a, b) = (descend2(s, f))(class(s, a), class(s, b)))))
- `descend2-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies descend2(s, f) in fun(cartesian(quotient(s), quotient(s)), z)))
- `proj-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies proj(s) in fun(pts(s), quotient(s)))
- `quotient-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies quotient(s) in set)
- `quotient-rep` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([x in quotient(s)], forsome([a in pts(s)], x = class(s, a))))
- `quotient-universal` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forsome([g in fun(quotient(s), z)], forall([a in pts(s)], g(class(s, a)) = f(a)) and forall([g_], g_ in fun(quotient(s), z) and forall([a in pts(s)], g_(class(s, a)) = f(a)) implies g_ = g))))
- `rko2-refl` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_ in pts(s)], related(s, a_, a_)))
- `rko2-sym` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, a_)))
- `rko2-trans` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_, c_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies c_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, c_) implies related(s, a_, c_)))

### top-space
<a id="top-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/top-space.scm`](../structure-library/top-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): carriers (pts), ops/constants (opens)

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure top-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers pts)
  (constant opens (power (power pts)))
  (law (in empty-set (opens s)))
  (law (in (pts s) (opens s)))
  (law (forall u (implies (in u (opens s)) (forall v (implies (in v (opens s)) (in (intersection u v) (opens s)))))))
  (law (forall fam (implies (subset fam (opens s)) (in (big-union u fam u) (opens s))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-top-space` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and opens(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and empty-set in opens(s) and pts(s) in opens(s) and forall([u in opens(s), v in opens(s)], intersection(u, v) in opens(s)) and forall([fam], fam subset opens(s) implies big-union(u, fam, u) in opens(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-top-space`.*

- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))
- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s) iff forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `hom-top-space-id` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hom-top-space(s, s, id-fun(pts(s))))
- `metrizable-iff-bounded-metrizable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `metrizable-iff-bounded-metrizable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-metrizable-top-space(s))
- `metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))
- `metrizable-iff-gauge-countable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-metrizable-top-space(s))

### vector-space
<a id="vector-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/finite-dimensional.scm`](../structure-library/finite-dimensional.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as module)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`module`](#module).  Shape inherited from `module` — slots (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure vector-space
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as module)
  (law "is-field-ring(scal(s))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-vector-space-def` — forall([s], is-vector-space(s) iff is-module(s) and is-field-ring(scal(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-vector-space`.*

- `hom-vector-space-id` — forall([a], is-vector-space(a) implies is-hom-vector-space(a, a, id-fun(vec(a))))
- `vspace-vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)

### zz-ring
<a id="zz-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/numeric-instances.scm`](../structure-library/numeric-instances.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`euclidean-ring`](#euclidean-ring).

*Member of:*

- `is-ring` (via `zz-is-ring`)
- `is-commutative-ring` (via `zz-is-commutative-ring`)
- `is-integral-domain` (via `zz-is-integral-domain`)
- `is-euclidean-ring` (via `zz-is-euclidean-ring`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `euclidean-ring` slots):

- `carr` = `zz`
- `add` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_)`
- `mul` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_)`
- `neg` = `vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_)`
- `zero` = `0`
- `one` = `1`

## View-as declarations (alphabetical)

A view-as is a *forgetful functor* between structure categories, in exactly two cases: it forgets **shape** (keeps a sub-list of the source's slots) and/or forgets **properties** (the target's laws are fewer than the source's).  The slot map below is written in the *target's* coordinates: position k shows which source accessor fills the target's k-th slot.  Non-forgetful functors (quotient, completion) are constructions, not views.

- `abelian-group-as-monoid` — `abelian-group` → `monoid`: (carr opr iden) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag` — `commutative-ring` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm` — `commutative-ring` → `comm-monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — `complex-inner-product-space` → `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)
- `field-additive-ag` — `field` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `field-as-euclidean-ring` — `field` → `euclidean-ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-as-integral-domain` — `field` → `integral-domain`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-multiplicative-group` — `field` → `group`: (non-zero mul one mul-inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `measure-space-as-measurable-space` — `measure-space` → `measurable-space`: (pts sigma) ↦ (pts sigma)
- `module-vector-ag` — `module` → `abelian-group`: (vec vadd vzero vneg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — `normed-ag` → `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-field-additive-ag` — `normed-field` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — `normed-field` → `commutative-ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `normed-field-as-integral-domain` — `normed-field` → `integral-domain`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `normed-vector-space-as-module` — `normed-vector-space` → `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)
- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — `normed-vector-space` → `normed-ag`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)
- `ring-additive-ag` — `ring` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — `ring` → `monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `ringoid-as-ring` — `ringoid` → `ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
